## Australia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Australia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 books, all of them student textbooks** (Years 8–10: eight Cambridge volumes — the Essential Mathematics Australian Curriculum, Victorian Curriculum and CambridgeMATHS NSW lines, plus the GOLD support edition and the CORE pathway volume — and one Jacaranda volume, Maths Quest 10+10A) — and placed 844 teaching items. Australia has no government textbook series (ACARA writes the Australian Curriculum but publishes and prescribes no textbooks); the set is the school-used commercial series the country records name, and every book is cited as such. All 9 files are distinct works (distinct ISBNs, extents and contents, verified per book on the read); the publisher-line relations are stated in the placements: Book A1 (GOLD) is the support edition of Book A2's Year 8 material, Book A3 is the same line in its Victorian ©2024 edition, Books A4 and A6 are the NSW syllabus editions, and Book A9 is Book A8's CORE pathway companion. In the sections below the books are cited as Books A1–A9. Australia COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every item is pupil-textbook teaching. The read's four flagged claims are carried exactly: (F1) Book A3's contents lists its Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the file in hand contains the full chapter, bound after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941) — it is read and placed, and it founds this course's algorithmic-thinking lesson; (F2) Book A7's contents lists a Chapter 18, Algorithmic thinking, with no page numbers, and the chapter is NOT in the file (searched end to end; the book closes with its Glossary, printed p. 1106, and Index, printed p. 1117) — recorded as absent, not read, and no item rests on it; (F3) Book A9's running head at printed p. 644 prints section 10E's code as “0E” — the book's own production error; the placement cites 10E, as the section's heading and contents give it; (F4) only the 9 Maths books are this read's set — the country records' wider Australia picture (12 staged books across subjects, plus filled-by-exact-link titles) is not merged into this count, and in particular the two senior Maths titles that exist only as exact-link records (Cambridge Senior Specialist Mathematics, QCE Units 3&4, 2e, and Cambridge Senior Mathematical Methods, QCE Units 3&4, 2e) are NOT in hand and were NOT read. The placements' named exclusions — front matter and “how to use this book” guides; every chapter's pre-tests, progress quizzes, unlettered applications / problem-solving / modelling / investigation spreads, puzzles and games, chapter summaries, checklists and reviews, semester reviews, glossaries, indexes and answer sections — are apparatus/assessment, teach no new content, and are stocked nowhere. The set is Years 8–10 secondary only, so the primary lessons (counting, place value, first steps) receive no Australia item — that is the set's shape, not a reading gap. Australia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (9/9) has been read and placed; the two senior QCE exact-link titles do not block the in-hand class (if exact-link titles are ever ruled in-scope for the courses reckoning, Australia drops to PARTIAL until they are grabbed and read). Into this lesson the placements stock 32 items from Book A1, Book A2, Book A3, Book A4, Book A5, Book A6, Book A7, Book A8, Book A9.

**Book A1:** *Cambridge Mathematics 8 (CambridgeMaths GOLD)* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2012, ISBN 978-1-107-64383-3; 62,774,078 bytes, 554 PDF pp. The GOLD support edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line, developed for students who require additional support in mathematics; a distinct work from Book A2 (different ISBN, extent and arrangement), not a duplicate file. ## Brazil added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Brazil's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a pupil textbook: *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio* (SEED-PR), the Paraná state public textbook for Ensino Médio (Grades 10–12), in Portuguese. There is no teacher guide in the Brazil Maths set in hand, so every Brazil row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Brazil counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 128 teaching items. The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher = 1” is correct: books.json (grade-10/gov/brazil) and textbooks.tsv each carry exactly one Brazil Mathematics entry, and phase3-push-state.json lists the file as pushed. The staging folder for the book is empty, so there was no local copy to read, and the authenticated GitHub asset route failed in this pass (the mid-size-books release's asset listing repeatedly disconnected through the proxy, and the asset's download URL answered 404) — the GitHub copy's existence and size were therefore NOT verified here, and a library-side check of that asset is owed (a route failure, not a negative finding). The book was instead read from its own recorded source_url — the Wayback capture banked by the 2026-10-22 CDX prep (capture 20240421222141) — which landed at exactly the prep's expected 6,379,928 B, with 240 pages and the correct cover and catalogue block; the book read is certainly this book. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Four book errors found in the read (placements E1–E4) are carried corrected on the rows they touch — in the sequences-and-series (E1), ratio-and-proportion (E2), coordinate-geometry (E3), barycentres (E4) and loci (E4) lessons; two further misprints are recorded, not taught (R5: the risk exponent typeset once as 0.2525 for 0.2528, printed p. 58 — noted on the item 28 row in the logarithms-and-exponentials lesson; R6: glucose printed “C6A12O6”, printed p. 35, a chemistry misprint), and the Aristarchus bound passage (printed p. 169, record item R7) could not be confirmed either way as extracted and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the four section introductions and the general presentation (framing and history), the context essays and debates that carry no new maths, the biographical and historical passages except where the passage itself teaches a method, the “Obras Consultadas” reference lists after every chapter, and the blank “Anotações” pages — are stocked nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items (all from the pupil textbook) from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio* (pupil textbook, Ensino Médio — Grades 10–12) — Brazil, Secretaria de Estado da Educação do Paraná (SEED-PR), Curitiba, 2nd edition 2006, ISBN 85-85380-39-X; 6,379,928 bytes, 240 PDF pp., MD5 e3dac71a6d075dd142282376da6fa785. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Read from the banked Wayback capture of the book's recorded source_url at the exact recorded size; the GitHub release-asset copy could not be verified in this pass (route failure — a library-side asset check is owed). The catalogue block's “p. 216” is the 1st-edition pagination and does not match this file's 240 pages (record item R1); all citations use the actual printed folios. Folha 6's chapter heading (printed p. 77) adds “Corrupção & Política –” to the contents-list title (record item R2) — the same chapter. Folhas 9 and 10 are filed by the book inside its Funções section although their content is trigonometry (record item R3); the placements follow the content.

|---|---|
| Rectangles with the same perimeter (P = 22 m) having different areas (18, 24, 28 m², …): with the perimeter fixed, the area varies with the base and height | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 43 |
| Rectangles with the area fixed at 64 m² and different bases and heights have different perimeters, tabulated and drawn on the Cartesian plane | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 44 |
| The greatest area for a given perimeter is obtained with a square — the shape that saves material | Brazil — *Matemática — Livro Didático Público — Ensino Médio (SEED-PR)* (pupil textbook), printed p. 45 |

## Bhutan added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bhutan's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **16 files: 13 student books and 3 teacher's guides of the Royal Government of Bhutan's *Understanding Mathematics* series (Department of School Education / Ministry of Education and Skills Development), English-medium throughout** — and placed 657 pupil teaching items and 30 teacher-evidence items. **Set correction, proved by the read: the expansion map's count of 10 student + 3 teacher is WRONG — the map counted only the textbooks.tsv snapshot (PP, I, II, IV–X) and missed the Class III, XI and XII student books recorded as secured and pushed in the staging books.json files and the push state; the true in-hand set is 13 student + 3 teacher = 16 files, every one grabbed and verified (byte size on every file; MD5 against books.json on the 11 files that carry one — all match; page counts match every record) and read in full.** The Class I staging-path collision is resolved: the main-branch grade-01 file is the student activity book (Book 3); the Class I teacher's guide (Book 4) is a separate, MD5-verified file that books.json records under the same staging path name. **Critical filing flag, carried exactly: both BHSEC filings claim "complete book, not a sample/preview" — FALSE. The Class XI file holds only chapters 1–5 of the 26 its own contents lists and the Class XII file only chapters 1–4 of the 19 its contents lists; only the chapters actually present were placed, and both books are cited as the partial excerpts they are.** The 3 teacher's guides are teacher evidence only: their items are named below as teacher evidence and are never counted as pupil teaching. The Class 1 activity book's Reproducible Pages (printed pp. 75–80) and Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (pp. 81–100) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class 2 activity book's Summative Assessment Recording Sheets (printed pp. 99–118) are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The pupil Chapter 9 pages open at Lesson 3 (Interpreting Bar Graphs) — no pupil Lessons 1–2 for that chapter appear in the file; the guide's parallel survey and data-collection lessons stand as teacher evidence instead. Book errors in the Class 2 book are carried corrected: its imprint prints the copyright year as "20234", read as 2023 as on its guide, and its Chapter 6 Lesson 6 tells pupils to draw a cylinder and label its base and apex — a cylinder has no apex; the apex belongs to the cone in the same lesson. The Class 3 textbook's Chapter Overviews, Chapter Reviews and course-objectives/assessment front matter are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The Class IV textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class V textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class VI textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its contents numbers two different sections 6.2.2 ("Bisectors" and "EXPLORE: Sorting Quadrilaterals"); the second is cited with the duplication named. The Class VII textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. The Class VIII textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its contents misprints §5.3.2 (Surface Area of a Rectangular Prism) at printed p. 157, after the Unit 5 Revision at p. 150; the sequence places it at ~p. 147 and the row cites it so. The Class X textbook's Getting Started spreads, GAME and CONNECTIONS features, Unit Revisions, Glossary, Measurement Reference and Answers are apparatus/practice and are stocked nowhere. Its assessment introduction carries a "curriculum for Class IX" line — a carry-over in the Class X book, read as Class X. Into this lesson the placements stock 24 items (24 pupil) from Books 3, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12 and 14.

## Cabo Verde added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cabo Verde’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 books, all of them student (pupil) manuals of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde: *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (Edição 2019), *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (Edição 2020) and *Matemática 7º ano — Manual do(a) aluno(a)* (Edição 2020); 0 teacher guides** — and placed 197 teaching items (Grade 3: 47, Grade 5: 69, Grade 7: 81; 391 PDF pages, every page read). The expansion map’s count of 3 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT. Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2019; authors Jimmy Leppãnen, Sara Hoye, FvZ; editor Gamlin & Zeipel Communication AB; 140,731,505 bytes, 130 PDF pp., MD5 112ca4f2fc6577b4eb035b0237d9253c (read from the GitHub release copy in release `full-quality-oversized-books`; its byte size equals the ministry HEAD size recorded in the download list §C row 13 exactly). Printed page = PDF page. Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2020; authors João Duarte, Vanda Delgado; 11,054,236 bytes, 118 PDF pp., MD5 7523a460a8d971a028632077eeb0789c (the GitHub copy and the ministry copy are byte-identical — one book, cited once). Printed page = PDF page throughout the body (its unit-opener pages, PDF pp. 6–7, are unnumbered and are cited by PDF page). Pupil textbook.

## Canada added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Canada's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **2 works in 4 files: *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (FNESC/FNSA, ©2020), 273 pp. — the one book in Canada's classified in-hand Maths set (0 student + 1 teacher) — and the borderline pupil work *I Like Math!* (Government of Nunavut, 2017), one Level 8 guided-reading fiction book held in three language editions of 11 pp. each and filed in every record under the Inuktut Ilinniaqta Early Books label, not under Maths** — and placed 117 items (112 teacher-guide evidence items from the guide, items 1–112, plus 5 pupil items from *I Like Math!*, items S1–S5). Every item from the guide is TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE: the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts, with mini-units, activities and blackline masters, and states BC curriculum connections in its tables; it expressly does not explain the mathematics or the curriculum in detail (its printed p. 35) and presumes a trained teacher. No pupil Maths textbook is in hand for Canada: Canada has no national textbook series (education is provincial/territorial), and the five provincial student textbooks in the records (Nelson *Principles of Mathematics 10*; McGraw-Hill Ryerson *Principles of Mathematics 9*; Pearson *Math Makes Sense 3, 7, 8*) stand filled-by-exact-link only — never staged, never secured, not read here and cited nowhere (a sixth, *Nelson Mathematics 8*, is a candidate only, its imprint never read). Canada is therefore cited as EVIDENCE ONLY, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent: Canada is added to this lesson's countries line and named in its rows, but counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure. The *I Like Math!* items are stocked as pupil evidence from that work exactly as the placements label them — 3 carry taught content (S1, S2, S4) and 2 are context/named-only (S3, S5) — and are likewise counted in no taught-in figure; whether the Nunavut early-books titles count as pupil Maths at all is a record-side question for the parent, reported here, not ruled on. The guide's Water-Keepers items (98–105) rest on the guide's printed teaching alone: the separate FNESC Water Keepers digital resource the guide points to (printed p. 224) is in no record as in hand and was not read. The guide is the 2020 revision of the 2011 *Math First Peoples* resource; the 2011 predecessor located on a Padlet host is not a separate in-hand book and is cited nowhere. The placements' named exclusions — the guide's introduction, goals/rationale and teacher-reflection material; First Peoples Principles of Learning, story protocols and respectful-practice rules; the resource-design rubric; community-visit apparatus beyond the Interview Guide's six content categories; unit-organization front matter; the cultural/historical context narratives and stories inside the mini-units (context-setting only; no story text is reproduced anywhere in the placements or here); BLM worksheets that merely repeat an itemised activity; formative/summative assessment prompts, guiding questions, video and website lists and the Additional Resources bibliographies; and the *I Like Math!* front/back matter — teach no new mathematics and are stocked nowhere. The 5 book errors found in the read (placements E1–E5) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: E1 (sphere-volume worked example, printed p. 123 — a unit slip and a garbled intermediate line inside a volume calculation; the final answer, 500 cm³, is correct) on item 56; E2 (BLM 6 conversion sheet, printed p. 185 — the percent-to-decimal rule states the decimal-point direction backwards; both printed examples are correct) on item 83; E3 (Moose Tracks answer key, printed p. 181 — mixes “less than or equal to 20” with m < 20 and marks the boundary value inconsistently) on item 71; E4 (Circle Unit Answer Key, printed p. 211 — says “smoke hole” for “fire circle”; the project instructions at printed p. 204 state it correctly) on item 92; E5 (Weaving BLM list, printed p. 55 — numbers both Keep or Toss sheets BLM 16; they are BLM 15 and BLM 16) is a list typo affecting no item's content. Printed page = PDF page in the guide throughout; *I Like Math!* citations are its printed story folios. Canada's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (items 9, 10, 16 and 86).

**Book 1:** *Math First Peoples — Teacher Resource Guide, Elementary & Secondary* (teacher resource guide) — Canada, First Nations Education Steering Committee (FNESC) and First Nations Schools Association (FNSA), ©2020; 32,264,979 bytes (byte-exact against the record), 273 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide directs teachers how to teach BC mathematics through First Peoples contexts and states curriculum connections; it expressly does not explain the mathematics in detail (printed p. 35); it is not a pupil textbook.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 78, 154, 198, 199 and 201).

Jorge Díaz Porras (contenidos de los nuevos programas 2015); 10,890,820 bytes, 425 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

## Cuba added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cuba's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **29 in-hand entries = 25 distinct works (MINED, Editorial Pueblo y Educación, Cuba), every one read in full**: student textbooks for Grades 1 and 3–12, student workbooks for Grades 1, 2 and 3, and teacher documents (Programa and Orientaciones metodológicas) for Grades 1–5, 8 and 11 — and placed 247 teaching items (202 student-side, 45 teacher-evidence). **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 14 student + 8 teacher is WRONG** — the staging books.json holds 13 student + 9 teacher entries and textbooks.tsv holds 7 further secured entries; duplicated filings are cited once (the Grade 3 and Grade 8 student books were each verified in a second edition; the Grade 4 and Grade 5 student books are each filed twice as byte-identical copies — Grade 4 MD5 01c106741b186cf28d360854a40d7e93, Grade 5 MD5 b48e28b1df820e2451bb44eb33b5ac11). Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. The placements' named exclusions — exercise sets, chapter exercises, self-evaluation sections, answers appendices, mathematical tables, historical feature essays and front/back matter — are stocked nowhere. Teacher items are TEACHER EVIDENCE — they state what the programme requires and how it is taught — are labelled as such on every row, and are never counted in this lesson's acceptance figure; student textbook and workbook items are COUNTED. Printed pages are the books' own printed folios as the placements cite them (each book's printed-page basis is stated in its book entry). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items (items 83, 96, 142, 152, 178): 4 student items and 1 teacher-evidence item.

## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items (items 48, 112, 113, 114).

FULL BOOK (112 lessons, contents to printed p. 140). Pupil textbook.

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. The book error found in the read that touches this lesson is carried corrected on the row it touches: the area-conversion worked answers on printed p. 127 print “5700 cm² = 5700 m²” (correct: 0,57 m²) and “1 dm² = 100 m²” in a worked step (correct: 1 m² = 100 dm²); the sheet's stated rule (printed p. 126, × or ÷ by 100 per step) is correct, and item 66 is placed on the corrected conversions (placements error E4). Into this lesson the placements stock 6 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an OPH supplementary teacher activity collection, expressly “not tied to any textbook”; Finland has no national textbook series.

TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — the record says “student”, grade-05; the document is an instructor's guide addressing grades 5–7. Book error carried corrected: PDF p. 3 gives a small car's average mass as “40 g” — impossible for a car; taught corrected (a small car is of the order of 1,000 kg). Its worked snow example (1.77 kg ÷ 0.0177 m² = 100 kg/m²) is correct.

**Book 3:** *Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla* (pupil workbook, aoe.1371) — Finland, Järvenpää, 2021, ISBN 978-952-94-4547-9; 3,056,839 bytes, 62 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page. Its model solutions (*Perusmatematiikkaa ohjelmoimalla — Malliratkaisut*, aoe.1371, 31 pp.) mirror it task by task, confirm every placement below, and are cited wherever this book is cited. Pure-programming tasks (strings, lists, passwords, game mechanics) are stocked nowhere — only tasks whose own content is mathematics.

The one book was read from staging in place: 5,990,840 bytes (= the books.json record), 169 PDF pages, MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929, and it is the COMPLETE book, not a partial excerpt — the contents page (PDF p. 7) lists all 30 units, every unit start was verified at its contents page, and the text runs printed pp. 1–158, ending with the final “Let Us Look Back” exercises (printed p. 158) and the back cover. Printed page = PDF page − 8 throughout (Unit 1 begins printed p. 1 = PDF p. 9). Book observation F1, carried exactly: the cover reads “Revised Edition 2004” (the staging books.json and the checklist follow the cover) while the imprint page (PDF p. 2) reads “© 2005 … Revised 2005” — one consistent scan of one book, so the edition is cited as “Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005”. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The placements' named exclusions — front matter (acknowledgements, foreword, preface, contents; PDF pp. 1–8); every end-of-unit REVIEW section and every “LET US LOOK BACK” evaluative set (after Units 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and at the end of Unit 30, printed pp. 156–158 — revision and assessment exercises that restate items already placed, no new skill taught); worked story problems and exercise sets inside the units (practice of the item taught on that page, not separate items); and the example exchange rates themselves (dated examples that differ between Units 20 and 21 by design; the taught skill stocked is the conversion method) — are stocked nowhere. Guyana COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these pupil-book rows. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 items from Book 1 (items 110, 111, 112, 123, 124, 125).

**Book 1:** *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (student book, Grade 5, Easy Path Series) — Guyana, Ministry of Education, Georgetown; Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005 / Revised 2005; 5,990,840 bytes, 169 PDF pp., MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929 (read from staging in place). Printed page = PDF page − 8. Pupil textbook.

| Finding a missing side length when the perimeter is known | Guyana — *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (student book), printed pp. 116–117 |

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon stocks only evidence rows in this lesson — teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement, with no pupil-textbook row — so it is named in the countries line and in the rows but is NOT added to this lesson's acceptance count. ## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 7 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1), Book 2 (TT2 GL), Book 3 (TT3), Book 4 (TT6).

**Book 1:** *Téacs & Trialacha 1 (Eagrán Nua)* — TT1 (pupil textbook) — The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon; Irish edition of *Text & Tests 1: First-Year Maths* by Paul Cooke, O.D. Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

**Book 2:** *Téacs & Trialacha 2, Gnáthleibhéal (Eagrán Nua)* — TT2 GL (pupil textbook) — The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon; Irish edition of *Text & Tests 2: Ordinary Level Maths* (English ©2019), ISBN 978-1-9997795-5-9; 23,039,404 bytes, 543 PDF pp. Junior Cycle Ordinary Level (two-year course). Printed page = PDF page − 9.

**Book 3:** *Téacs & Trialacha 3* — TT3 (pupil textbook) — The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon; Leaving Certificate Ordinary Level, Strands 1–5; English ©2012, Irish © Foras na Gaeilge 2013, ISBN 978-1-85791-993-6; 11,385,583 bytes, 604 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 10. **The held file is the updated edition (2024): its preface states it is the completed edition of the 2013 book with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics — one chapter more than the library records describe (the read's flag F2); Chapters 1–18 are the recorded book.**

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 23 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 3 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 4 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 5 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 6 (*Mathematics Year 8*), Book 7 (*Form 3 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 8 (*Mathematics for Year 11*), Book 9 (*Year 12 Mathematics Textbook*).

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Year 3* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 37,315,079 bytes, 80 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Year 4* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 96,814,549 bytes, 86 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Folios cited as printed in the book; the folio sequence is irregular (folios 44–48 and 54 never appear, and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice) — the leaf count closes exactly and every contents strand and unit is present, so the gaps are the book's own folio misprints, not missing leaves.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 13 items — 11 pupil-textbook rows and 2 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 3 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 5to grado*), Book 4 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 6to grado*), Book 10 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 5to grado*).

**Book 1:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (the PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support); 16,407,713 bytes, 202 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (PROMETAM / UPNFM series, JICA support); 11,628,453 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 5to grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (PROMETAM / UPNFM series, JICA support); 29,854,150 bytes, 148 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 4:** *Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 6to grado* — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación (PROMETAM / UPNFM series, JICA support); 19,879,660 bytes, 172 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page − 6. Pupil textbook.

**Book 10:** *Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 5to grado* (teacher guide) — Honduras, Secretaría de Educación; 16,966,239 bytes, 198 PDF pp. Teacher evidence only.

## Italy added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Italy's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 pupil textbooks, all adopted commercial titles (Italy has no government textbooks; schools adopt titles under the AIE/MIUR adozioni system): the Bergamini *Matematica.blu* volumes (Zanichelli) and the Sasso *Colori della Matematica* Edizione Verde and Edizione Blu volumes and triennio modules A–G (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola)** — and placed 781 items. The expansion map's “14 student + 0 teacher” count and kind split are exact against the staging `books.json` files, `textbooks.tsv` and the push state; every file was verified against its record (byte size, and MD5 where the record carries one) and read in full. Counting grain: one item per numbered theory section of the book's own Indice/Sommario. Two extent faults ride with the set, honestly: the files filed as *Matematica.blu* Vol. 1 (Book 3) and *Matematica.blu 2.0* Vol. 5 (Book 4) are each only the SECOND PART of the volume — Vol. 1 opens at Capitolo 13 (printed p. 670) and its Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file; Vol. 5 opens at Capitolo 25 (printed p. 1560) and its Capitoli 18–24 are not in the file — so both are named as partial volumes wherever they are cited, only their held content is placed, and the missing first parts are not in hand anywhere and are cited nowhere (the ground the Vol. 1 first part would have covered is supplied inside this set by Books 5–6 at Sasso depth, and the Vol. 5 first part's limits and continuity ground by Book 12, Modulo G). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the eBook-only units the books' own Indici mark “(solo nell'eBook)”; and the apparatus around the numbered theory sections in every book — chapter openings, exercise blocks, laboratories, *Realtà e modelli*, exam-preparation sections (*Verso l'esame*, *Verso l'INVALSI*, *Verso l'università*), glossaries, answer keys, formula sheets, and *Approfondimento* boxes that are not numbered theory sections. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 2*), Book 3 (*Matematica.blu — Vol. 1*, second part only) and Book 7 (*Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 2*).

**Book 1:** Bergamini/Trifone/Barozzi, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 2: Algebra, Geometria, Probabilità* (Zanichelli, 2010; filed at Grade 10) — Italy; 12,160,155 bytes, 907 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published: the volume opens at Capitolo 9 (printed p. 633), its pagination continuous from Vol. 1. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 3:** Bergamini/Barozzi/Trifone, *Matematica.blu — Vol. 1* (Zanichelli, 2016; filed at Grade 9) — Italy; 54,032,035 bytes, 909 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. **Partial volume as held — the file is the volume's SECOND PART only:** Algebra Capitoli 13–21, Capitolo β and Geometry Capitoli G5–G11, its theory opening at printed p. 670; Capitoli 1–12 and G1–G4 are not in the file and are cited nowhere. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).

**Book 7:** Sasso/Zanone, *Colori della Matematica — Edizione Blu, Vol. 2* (Petrini / De Agostini Scuola, 2017, ISBN 9788849421675; filed at Grade 10) — Italy; 89,032,939 bytes, 902 PDF pp., MD5 identical to the source record's. Complete as published. Pupil textbook (adopted commercial title).
